- **amr-rebuttal**: Use when writing the response document for an Academy of Management Review (AMR) Revise & Resubmit — structuring point-by-point replies that show the theory was genuinely strengthened, not just defended. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Academy-of-Management-Review-Skills/skills/amr-rebuttal) · ★1.1k)
- **amr-review-process**: Use when you need to understand the Academy of Management Review (AMR) developmental, multi-round review and interpret a decision letter focused on theoretical novelty and logical soundness. Explains the process and decision types; it does NOT draft the response document (that is amr-rebuttal). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Academy-of-Management-Review-Skills/skills/amr-review-process) · ★1.1k)
- **amr-submission**: Use when running the final pre-submission preflight for an Academy of Management Review (AMR) manuscript — ScholarOne portal, AOM format and length, abstract, anonymization for review, references, ethics/originality, and required files. Theory-only journal; there is no data-availability or replication step. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Academy-of-Management-Review-Skills/skills/amr-submission) · ★1.1k)
- **amr-tables-figures**: Use when building the conceptual exhibits of an Academy of Management Review (AMR) manuscript — process models, 2x2 typologies, multi-level frameworks, and propositions tables that carry theoretical work. These are CONCEPTUAL figures, NOT data plots; AMR has no charts of results because it has no data. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Academy-of-Management-Review-Skills/skills/amr-tables-figures) · ★1.1k)
- **amr-theory-development**: Use when building the actual theory for an Academy of Management Review (AMR) manuscript — turning a positioned puzzle into defined constructs, explicit relationships, propositions, and boundary conditions. Constructs the theory; it does NOT stress-test the argument's logic (that is amr-data-analysis) or design any data collection (AMR has none). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Academy-of-Management-Review-Skills/skills/amr-theory-development) · ★1.1k)
- **amr-topic-selection**: Use when the theoretical puzzle is the bottleneck for an Academy of Management Review (AMR) manuscript — deciding whether an idea is theory-worthy and AMR-fit before any theorizing begins. Frames the puzzle; it does NOT build constructs (that is amr-theory-development) or design any empirical study (AMR has none). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Academy-of-Management-Review-Skills/skills/amr-topic-selection) · ★1.1k)
- **amr-workflow**: Use when deciding which amr-* sub-skill to invoke next, or when sequencing a theory-building manuscript from theoretical-puzzle framing through developmental-review revision for an Academy of Management Review (AMR) submission. Routes — does not replace — the specialized skills. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Academy-of-Management-Review-Skills/skills/amr-workflow) · ★1.1k)
- **amr-writing-style**: Use when polishing the prose and structure of an Academy of Management Review (AMR) manuscript to AOM house style and an argument-driven voice. - **amstar2-review-appraisal**: Appraises a systematic review or meta-analysis with AMSTAR 2 (AMSTAR-2; Shea et al., BMJ 2017): answers the 16 checklist items from the review's report, flags flaws in the seven critical domains and derives the overall confidence rating — High, Moderate, Low or Critically low — never a summed score. Aciona: analisar CNAE, qual o anexo do Simples, vedacao ao Simples, atividade permite o regime, ISS ou ICMS, percentual de presuncao, Fator R, RAT/FAP, enquadramento de ati… ([sbroggioadv/auditoria-contabil-os-marketplace](https://github.com/sbroggioadv/auditoria-contabil-os-marketplace/tree/HEAD/auditoria-contabil-os/skills/analise-cnae-atividades) · ★1)
- **analise-cnis**: ANALISE DE CNIS — Skill Tier 2 C. Le e analisa Cadastro Nacional de Informacoes Sociais (CNIS) do segurado, mapeando vinculos empregaticios, contribuicoes, periodos concomitantes, lacunas, pendencias administrativas (PE, PVI, IREC), e integridade do registro. Output: mapa de tempo de contribuicao bruto + carencia + sinalizacao de problemas. ALICERCE de qualquer demanda previdenciaria — PA-13 obriga analise antes de qualquer peca/calculo. Use quando operador anexar CNIS ou pedir analise de tempo, vinculos, ou conferencia de dados previdenciarios. ([sbroggioadv/previdenciario-adv-os-marketplace](https://github.com/sbroggioadv/previdenciario-adv-os-marketplace/tree/HEAD/previdenciario-adv-os/skills/analise-cnis) · ★2)

- **analise-critica-de-pecas-criativas**: Avaliar peças criativas com visão estratégica — identificando pontos fortes, fracos e melhorias práticas. ([tiagopgr/skills-ia](https://github.com/tiagopgr/skills-ia/tree/HEAD/skills/direcao-criativa/skills/analise-critica-de-pecas-criativas) · ★11)

- **analise-de-casos-com-recomendacoes-praticas**: Analisar casos e sugerir estratégias e próximos passos — com foco em ação. ([tiagopgr/skills-ia](https://github.com/tiagopgr/skills-ia/tree/HEAD/skills/juridico-advocacia/skills/analise-de-casos-com-recomendacoes-praticas) · ★11)
- **analise-de-causa-raiz-de-falha-operacional**: Investigar falhas, erros e não-conformidades operacionais usando metodologia estruturada — identificando a causa raiz real, não apenas o sintoma visível — e gerar um plano de ação corretivo com responsável e prazo. - **analise-de-roi-e-apresentacao-de-resultados**: Calcular e apresentar o ROI (Retorno sobre Investimento) de uma campanha de marketing de forma clara, conectada ao resultado de negócio do cliente e estruturada para demonstrar o valor do trabalho realizado. Transforma números de campanha em linguagem de negócio — quanto foi investido, quanto gerou, qual o retorno — respondendo a pergunta que todo cliente tem mas nem sempre faz. - **analise-de-roi-implementacao-de-ia**: Calcular e apresentar de forma clara e convincente o retorno sobre investimento de uma implementação de IA — com estimativa de tempo economizado, custo evitado, receita recuperada e payback do investimento — em uma tabela executiva que o empresário entende e que resolve a objeção "não sei se vale a pena". O ROI bem calculado vende mais do que qualquer argumento técnico. ([tiagopgr/skills-ia](https://github.com/tiagopgr/skills-ia/tree/HEAD/skills/financeiro/skills/analise-de-roi-implementacao-de-ia) · ★11)
- **analise-de-viabilidade-de-investimento-para-cliente**: Analisar a viabilidade financeira de investimentos e decisões estratégicas de clientes — calculando payback, retorno esperado, ponto de equilíbrio do investimento e cenários de risco — para que o consultor ajude o cliente a tomar decisões de alocação de capital com base em dados, e não em intuição ou pressão do mercado. ([tiagopgr/skills-ia](https://github.com/tiagopgr/skills-ia/tree/HEAD/skills/financeiro/skills/analise-de-viabilidade-de-investimento-para-cliente) · ★11)
- **analise-de-viabilidade-de-projeto**: Avaliar rapidamente se uma oportunidade de projeto vale o investimento de tempo e recurso para elaborar proposta — usando um framework de qualificação que analisa potencial financeiro, viabilidade técnica, alinhamento estratégico e probabilidade de fechamento — para que o gestor pare de desperdiçar horas em propostas de baixa probabilidade e foque energia nos projetos certos, aumentando a taxa de conversão sem aumentar o esforço comercial.
